# Test source

```ts
  1   | /** Checks the files a campaign shows, before and after it runs. */
  2   | 
  3   | import { scanConfigResults } from '@locators/scan-config';
  4   | import { expect, type Locator, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
  5   | 
  6   | import { NO_NAVIGATION } from '../interactions';
  7   | import type { ScanConfigCase, ScanConfigView } from '../scan-config';
  8   | 
  9   | const LOG_FILE = 'Task logs';
  10  | 
  11  | /**
  12  |  * How long a file list may take to settle once the run has finished. The
  13  |  * outputs are fetched one by one after "done", and a circuit's polls for its
  14  |  * visualization for twenty seconds.
  15  |  */
  16  | const FILES_SETTLE_WITHIN = 60_000;
  17  | 
  18  | async function checkFiles(panel: Locator, names: string[]): Promise<void> {
  19  |   const files = panel.locator('[data-file-name]');
  20  | 
  21  |   // Comparing the names, not the count, so a changed output prints what changed.
  22  |   // A bounded retry: `toPass()` alone retries until the test runs out of clock,
  23  |   // so a list that will never match held an optimization's page for half an hour.
  24  |   await expect(async () => {
  25  |     const shown = await files.evaluateAll((items) =>
  26  |       items.map((item) => item.getAttribute('data-file-name') ?? '')
  27  |     );
  28  |     expect(shown.toSorted()).toEqual(names.toSorted());
  29  |   }).toPass({ timeout: FILES_SETTLE_WITHIN });
  30  | 
  31  |   for (const name of names) {
  32  |     await expect(panel.locator(`[data-file-name="${name}"]`)).toBeVisible();
  33  |   }
  34  | }
  35  | 
  36  | /** Checks the input and output files listed right after generation. */
  37  | export async function checkGeneratedFiles(
  38  |   page: Page,
  39  |   configuration: ScanConfigCase
  40  | ): Promise<void> {
  41  |   const results = scanConfigResults(page);
  42  |   const generated = configuration.expect.generated;
  43  | 
  44  |   // A workflow nobody has run through names no files, so it only has to hold some.
  45  |   if (!generated) {
  46  |     await expect(results.inputs.locator('[data-file-name]')).not.toHaveCount(0);
  47  |     return;
  48  |   }
  49  | 
  50  |   await checkFiles(results.inputs, generated.inputs);
  51  |   await checkFiles(results.outputs, generated.outputs);
  52  | }
  53  | 
  54  | /** Checks the files a finished campaign holds, and what each one shows. */
  55  | export async function checkCompletedOutput(
  56  |   page: Page,
  57  |   configuration: ScanConfigCase
  58  | ): Promise<void> {
  59  |   const completed = configuration.expect.completed;
  60  |   if (!completed) return;
  61  | 
  62  |   const results = scanConfigResults(page);
  63  | 
  64  |   await checkFiles(results.inputs, completed.inputs);
  65  |   await checkFiles(results.outputs, completed.outputs);
  66  | 
  67  |   if (completed.outputs.includes(LOG_FILE)) {
  68  |     await results.file(LOG_FILE).click(NO_NAVIGATION);
  69  |     await expect(results.logs).toContainText('Task execution completed.');
> 70  |     await expect(results.preview.entity.card).toHaveCount(0);
      |                                              ^ Error: expect(locator).toHaveCount(expected) failed
  71  |   }
  72  | 
  73  |   for (const [file, shown] of Object.entries(completed.views ?? {})) {
  74  |     await results.file(file).click(NO_NAVIGATION);
  75  |     await checkView(page, shown);
  76  |   }
  77  | }
  78  | 
  79  | /** A list is the text a pane holds; a mapping is the card of the entity a run registered. */
  80  | async function checkView(page: Page, shown: ScanConfigView): Promise<void> {
  81  |   const results = scanConfigResults(page);
  82  |
```
